fn compound_matches<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compound: &[Component<'input>],
    local_name: Option<&str>,
) -> bool {
    for component in compound {
        match component {
            Component::LocalName(name) => {
                let element_name = local_name.unwrap_or(element.local_name().as_str());
                if element_name != name.lower_name.as_ref() {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::ID(ident) => {
                if get_attribute!(element, Id).is_none_or(|id| id.0.as_str() != ident.as_ref()) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::Class(ident) => {
                if !element.has_class(ident.as_ref()) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { local_name, .. } => {
                let local_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
                if element.get_attribute_local(&local_name).is_none() {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::AttributeInNoNamespace {
                local_name,
                operator,
                value,
                case_sensitivity,
                ..
            } => {
                let attr_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
                let Some(attr) = element.get_attribute_local(&attr_name) else {
                    return false;
                };
                let value_str = attr
                    .value()
                    .to_value_string(PrinterOptions::default())
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                if !operator.eval_str(
                    &value_str,
                    value,
                    case_sensitivity.to_unconditional(false),
                ) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            // Universal selectors and namespace prefixes are ignored: changing a
            // shape's local-name never affects whether those components match.
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    true
}

fn matches_slot<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    local_name: Option<&str>,
) -> bool {
    if !compound_matches(element, &compounds[slot], local_name) {
        return false;
    }
    matches_right(element, compounds, combinators, slot)
        && matches_left(element, compounds, combinators, slot)
}

fn matches_right<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
) -> bool {
    if slot == 0 {
        return true;
    }
    let next = slot - 1;
    match combinators[slot - 1] {
        Combinator::Child => element.children_iter().any(|child| {
            compound_matches(&child, &compounds[next], None)
                && matches_right(&child, compounds, combinators, next)
        }),
        Combinator::Descendant => element.breadth_first().any(|descendant| {
            compound_matches(&descendant, &compounds[next], None)
                && matches_right(&descendant, compounds, combinators, next)
        }),
        Combinator::NextSibling => element.next_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], None)
                && matches_right(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next)
        }),
        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
            let mut sibling = element.next_element_sibling();
            while let Some(current) = sibling {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], None)
                    && matches_right(&current, compounds, combinators, next)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                sibling = current.next_element_sibling();
            }
            false
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn matches_left<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
) -> bool {
    if slot + 1 == compounds.len() {
        return true;
    }
    let next = slot + 1;
    match combinators[slot] {
        Combinator::Child => element.parent_element().is_some_and(|parent| {
            compound_matches(&parent, &compounds[next], None)
                && matches_left(&parent, compounds, combinators, next)
        }),
        Combinator::Descendant => {
            let mut ancestor = element.parent_element();
            while let Some(current) = ancestor {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], None)
                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                ancestor = current.parent_element();
            }
            false
        }
        Combinator::NextSibling => element.previous_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], None)
                && matches_left(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next)
        }),
        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
            let mut sibling = element.previous_element_sibling();
            while let Some(current) = sibling {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], None)
                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                sibling = current.previous_element_sibling();
            }
            false
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn elements_at_slot<'input, 'arena>(
    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    local_name: Option<&str>,
) -> Vec<Element<'input, 'arena>> {
    root.breadth_first()
        .filter(|element| matches_slot(element, compounds, combinators, slot, local_name))
        .collect()
}

fn analyze_selector<'input, 'arena>(
    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    selector: &lightningcss::selector::Selector<'input>,
    protected: &mut HashSet<usize>,
    fallback: &mut ReferencedShapes,
) {
    let mut iter = selector.iter();
    let mut compounds: Vec<Vec<Component<'input>>> = Vec::new();
    let mut combinators: Vec<Combinator> = Vec::new();
    loop {
        compounds.push(iter.by_ref().cloned().collect());
        match iter.next_sequence() {
            Some(combinator) => combinators.push(combinator),
            None => break,
        }
    }

    let supported_combinator = |combinator: &Combinator| {
        matches!(
            combinator,
            Combinator::Child
                | Combinator::Descendant
                | Combinator::NextSibling
                | Combinator::LaterSibling
        )
    };
    if !combinators.iter().all(supported_combinator)
        || !compounds
            .iter()
            .all(|compound| compound.iter().all(is_supported_component))
    {
        for compound in &compounds {
            for component in compound {
                if let Component::LocalName(name) = component {
                    if let Some(bit) = shape_bit(&name.lower_name) {
                        fallback.insert(bit);
                    }
                    if name.lower_name.as_ref() == "path" {
                        fallback.insert(ReferencedShapes::Path);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    for (slot, compound) in compounds.iter().enumerate() {
        for component in compound {
            let Component::LocalName(name) = component else {
                continue;
            };
            let lower = name.lower_name.as_ref();
            if shape_bit(lower).is_some() {
                // A currently-matching shape occupies this slot. Converting it
                // would break the selector, so protect every such element.
                for element in elements_at_slot(root, &compounds, &combinators, slot, None) {
                    if element.local_name().as_str() == lower {
                        protected.insert(element.id());
                    }
                }
            }
            if lower == "path" {
                // A shape would newly occupy this slot after being converted to
                // a path, creating a match that did not exist before.
                for element in elements_at_slot(root, &compounds, &combinators, slot, Some("path"))
                {
                    if shape_bit(element.local_name().as_str()).is_some() {
                        protected.insert(element.id());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

impl<'input> lightningcss::visitor::Visitor<'input> for State<'_, 'input, '_> {
    type Error = JobsError<'input>;


STEP 154 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
path = 'crates/oxvg_optimiser/src/jobs/convert_shape_to_path.rs'
src = open(path).read()

start = src.index('fn compound_matches')
end = src.index('impl<\'input> lightningcss::visitor::Visitor')
old = src[start:end]

new = r'''#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum TypeMode {
    /// Match against the element's actual local-name.
    Real,
    /// Match the anchor slot as if the element were already a `path`.
    AsPath,
    /// Match other slots as they will be after the rewrite: `path` type
    /// selectors are ignored (they may match once converted shapes become
    /// paths), while other type selectors are enforced.
    IgnorePath,
}

fn compound_matches<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compound: &[Component<'input>],
    mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    for component in compound {
        match component {
            Component::LocalName(name) => {
                let selector_name = name.lower_name.as_ref();
                let matches = match mode {
                    TypeMode::Real => element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name,
                    TypeMode::AsPath => selector_name == "path",
                    TypeMode::IgnorePath => {
                        selector_name == "path"
                            || element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name
                    }
                };
                if !matches {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::ID(ident) => {
                if get_attribute!(element, Id).is_none_or(|id| id.0.as_str() != ident.as_ref()) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::Class(ident) => {
                if !element.has_class(ident.as_ref()) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { local_name, .. } => {
                let local_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
                if element.get_attribute_local(&local_name).is_none() {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::AttributeInNoNamespace {
                local_name,
                operator,
                value,
                case_sensitivity,
                ..
            } => {
                let attr_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
                let Some(attr) = element.get_attribute_local(&attr_name) else {
                    return false;
                };
                let value_str = attr
                    .value()
                    .to_value_string(PrinterOptions::default())
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                if !operator.eval_str(
                    &value_str,
                    value,
                    case_sensitivity.to_unconditional(false),
                ) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            // Universal selectors and namespace prefixes are ignored: changing a
            // shape's local-name never affects whether those components match.
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    true
}

fn matches_slot<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    if !compound_matches(element, &compounds[slot], anchor_mode) {
        return false;
    }
    matches_right(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
        && matches_left(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
}

fn matches_right<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    if slot == 0 {
        return true;
    }
    let next = slot - 1;
    match combinators[slot - 1] {
        Combinator::Child => element.children_iter().any(|child| {
            compound_matches(&child, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_right(&child, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::Descendant => element.breadth_first().any(|descendant| {
            compound_matches(&descendant, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_right(&descendant, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::NextSibling => element.next_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_right(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
            let mut sibling = element.next_element_sibling();
            while let Some(current) = sibling {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                    && matches_right(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                sibling = current.next_element_sibling();
            }
            false
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn matches_left<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    if slot + 1 == compounds.len() {
        return true;
    }
    let next = slot + 1;
    match combinators[slot] {
        Combinator::Child => element.parent_element().is_some_and(|parent| {
            compound_matches(&parent, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_left(&parent, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::Descendant => {
            let mut ancestor = element.parent_element();
            while let Some(current) = ancestor {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                ancestor = current.parent_element();
            }
            false
        }
        Combinator::NextSibling => element.previous_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_left(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
            let mut sibling = element.previous_element_sibling();
            while let Some(current) = sibling {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                sibling = current.previous_element_sibling();
            }
            false
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn elements_at_slot<'input, 'arena>(
    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> Vec<Element<'input, 'arena>> {
    root.breadth_first()
        .filter(|element| {
            matches_slot(
                element,
                compounds,
                combinators,
                slot,
                anchor_mode,
                other_mode,
            )
        })
        .collect()
}

#[expect(clippy::needless_pass_by_value)]
fn lp_px(lp: cell::Ref<LengthPercentage>) -> Option<f64> {
    use lightningcss::values::length::LengthPercentage;
    match &lp.0 {
        LengthPercentage::Dimension(d) => d.to_px().map(|px| px as f64),
        _ => None,
    }
}
fn r_px(r: cell::Ref<Radius>) -> Option<f64> {
    cell::Ref::filter_map(r, |r| match r {
        Radius::LengthPercentage(lp) => Some(lp),
        Radius::Auto => None,
    })
    .ok()
    .and_then(lp_px)
}

        }) else {

impl<'input> lightningcss::visitor::Visitor<'input> for State<'_, 'input, '_> {
    type Error = JobsError<'input>;

    fn visit_types(&self) -> lightningcss::visitor::VisitTypes {
        visit_types!(SELECTORS)
    }

    fn visit_selector(
        &mut self,
        selector: &mut lightningcss::selector::Selector<'input>,
    ) -> Result<(), Self::Error> {
        analyze_selector(
            &self.root,
            selector,
            &mut self.protected,
            &mut self.fallback,
        );
        Ok(())
    }
}

analyze = r'''fn analyze_selector<'input, 'arena>(
    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    selector: &lightningcss::selector::Selector<'input>,
    protected: &mut HashSet<usize>,
    fallback: &mut ReferencedShapes,
) {
    let mut iter = selector.iter();
    let mut compounds: Vec<Vec<Component<'input>>> = Vec::new();
    let mut combinators: Vec<Combinator> = Vec::new();
    loop {
        compounds.push(iter.by_ref().cloned().collect());
        match iter.next_sequence() {
            Some(combinator) => combinators.push(combinator),
            None => break,
        }
    }

    let supported_combinator = |combinator: &Combinator| {
        matches!(
            combinator,
            Combinator::Child
                | Combinator::Descendant
                | Combinator::NextSibling
                | Combinator::LaterSibling
        )
    };
    if !combinators.iter().all(supported_combinator)
        || !compounds
            .iter()
            .all(|compound| compound.iter().all(is_supported_component))
    {
        for compound in &compounds {
            for component in compound {
                if let Component::LocalName(name) = component {
                    if let Some(bit) = shape_bit(&name.lower_name) {
                        fallback.insert(bit);
                    }
                    if name.lower_name.as_ref() == "path" {
                        fallback.insert(ReferencedShapes::Path);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    for (slot, compound) in compounds.iter().enumerate() {
        for component in compound {
            let Component::LocalName(name) = component else {
                continue;
            };
            let lower = name.lower_name.as_ref();
            if shape_bit(lower).is_some() {
                // A currently-matching shape occupies this slot. Converting it
                // would break the selector, so protect every such element.
                for element in elements_at_slot(
                    root,
                    &compounds,
                    &combinators,
                    slot,
                    TypeMode::Real,
                    TypeMode::Real,
                ) {
                    if element.local_name().as_str() == lower {
                        protected.insert(element.id());
                    }
                }
            }
            if lower == "path" {
                // A shape would newly occupy this slot after being converted to
                // a path, creating a match that did not exist before.
                for element in elements_at_slot(
                    root,
                    &compounds,
                    &combinators,
                    slot,
                    TypeMode::AsPath,
                    TypeMode::IgnorePath,
                ) {
                    if shape_bit(element.local_name().as_str()).is_some() {
                        protected.insert(element.id());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

use lightningcss::{
    printer::PrinterOptions,
    selector::{Combinator, Component},

#[cfg_attr(feature = "wasm", derive(Tsify))]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "napi", napi(object))]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "serde", derive(Serialize, Deserialize))]
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "serde", serde(rename_all = "camelCase"))]
/// Converts basic shapes to `<path>` elements
///
/// # Differences to SVGO
///
/// OXVG will avoid converting shapes which may be referenced by local-name
/// in stylesheets.
///
/// # Correctness
///
/// Rounding errors may cause slight changes in visual appearance.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Never.
///
/// If this job produces an error or panic, please raise an [issue](https://github.com/noahbald/oxvg/issues)
pub struct ConvertShapeToPath {
    /// Whether to convert `<circle>` and `<ellipses>` to paths.
    #[cfg_attr(feature = "serde", serde(default = "default_convert_arcs"))]
    pub convert_arcs: bool,
    /// The number of decimal places to round to
    #[cfg_attr(feature = "wasm", tsify(type = "null | false | number"))]
    #[cfg_attr(feature = "serde", serde(default = "ConvertPrecision::default"))]
    pub float_precision: ConvertPrecision,
}

impl Default for ConvertShapeToPath {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            convert_arcs: default_convert_arcs(),
            float_precision: ConvertPrecision::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl<'input, 'arena> Visitor<'input, 'arena> for ConvertShapeToPath {
    type Error = JobsError<'input>;

    fn prepare(
        &self,
        document: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
        context: &mut Context<'input, 'arena, '_>,
    ) -> Result<oxvg_ast::visitor::PrepareOutcome, Self::Error> {
        context.query_has_stylesheet(document);
        let mut state = State {
            options: self,
            root: document.clone(),
            protected: HashSet::new(),
            fallback: ReferencedShapes::empty(),
        };
        for styles in &context.query_has_stylesheet_result {
            styles.borrow_mut().0.visit(&mut state)?;
        }
        state.start_with_context(document, context)?;
        Ok(PrepareOutcome::skip)
    }
}

struct State<'o, 'input, 'arena> {
    options: &'o ConvertShapeToPath,
    root: Element<'input, 'arena>,
    protected: HashSet<usize>,
    fallback: ReferencedShapes,
}

bitflags! {
    #[derive(Debug)]
    pub struct ReferencedShapes: usize {
        const Rect = 1 << 0;
        const Line = 1 << 1;
        const Polyline = 1 << 2;
        const Polygon = 1 << 3;
        const Circle = 1 << 4;
        const Ellipse = 1 << 5;
        const Path = 1 << 6;
    }
}

fn shape_bit(name: &str) -> Option<ReferencedShapes> {
    match name {
        "rect" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Rect),
        "line" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Line),
        "polyline" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Polyline),
        "polygon" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Polygon),
        "circle" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Circle),
        "ellipse" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Ellipse),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn is_supported_component(component: &Component<'_>) -> bool {
    matches!(
        component,
        Component::LocalName(_)
            | Component::ExplicitUniversalType

            | Component::ExplicitAnyNamespace
            | Component::ExplicitNoNamespace
            | Component::DefaultNamespace(_)
            | Component::Namespace(..)
            | Component::ID(_)
            | Component::Class(_)
            | Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { .. }
            | Component::AttributeInNoNamespace { .. }
    )
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum TypeMode {
    /// Match against the element's actual local-name.
    Real,
    /// Match the anchor slot as if the element were already a `path`.
    AsPath,
    /// Match other slots as they will be after the rewrite: `path` type
    /// selectors are ignored (they may match once converted shapes become
    /// paths), while other type selectors are enforced.
    IgnorePath,
}

fn compound_matches<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compound: &[Component<'input>],
    mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    for component in compound {
        match component {
            Component::LocalName(name) => {
                let selector_name = name.lower_name.as_ref();
                let matches = match mode {
                    TypeMode::Real => element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name,
                    TypeMode::AsPath => selector_name == "path",
                    TypeMode::IgnorePath => {
                        selector_name == "path"
                            || element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name
                    }
                };
                if !matches {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::ID(ident) => {
                if get_attribute!(element, Id).is_none_or(|id| id.0.as_str() != ident.as_ref()) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::Class(ident) => {
                if !element.has_class(ident.as_ref()) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { local_name, .. } => {
                let local_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
                if element.get_attribute_local(&local_name).is_none() {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            Component::AttributeInNoNamespace {
                local_name,
                operator,
                value,
                case_sensitivity,
                ..
            } => {
                let attr_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
                let Some(attr) = element.get_attribute_local(&attr_name) else {
                    return false;
                };
                let value_str = attr
                    .value()
                    .to_value_string(PrinterOptions::default())
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                if !operator.eval_str(
                    &value_str,
                    value,
                    case_sensitivity.to_unconditional(false),
                ) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            // Universal selectors and namespace prefixes are ignored: changing a
            // shape's local-name never affects whether those components match.
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    true
}

fn matches_slot<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    if !compound_matches(element, &compounds[slot], anchor_mode) {
        return false;
    }
    matches_right(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
        && matches_left(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
}

fn matches_right<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    if slot == 0 {
        return true;
    }
    let next = slot - 1;
    match combinators[slot - 1] {
        Combinator::Child => element.children_iter().any(|child| {
            compound_matches(&child, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_right(&child, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::Descendant => element.breadth_first().any(|descendant| {
            compound_matches(&descendant, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_right(&descendant, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::NextSibling => element.next_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_right(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
            let mut sibling = element.next_element_sibling();
            while let Some(current) = sibling {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                    && matches_right(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                sibling = current.next_element_sibling();
            }
            false
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn matches_left<'input, 'arena>(
    element: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> bool {
    if slot + 1 == compounds.len() {
        return true;
    }
    let next = slot + 1;
    match combinators[slot] {
        Combinator::Child => element.parent_element().is_some_and(|parent| {
            compound_matches(&parent, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_left(&parent, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::Descendant => {
            let mut ancestor = element.parent_element();
            while let Some(current) = ancestor {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                ancestor = current.parent_element();
            }
            false
        }
        Combinator::NextSibling => element.previous_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                && matches_left(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
        }),
        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
            let mut sibling = element.previous_element_sibling();
            while let Some(current) = sibling {
                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                sibling = current.previous_element_sibling();
            }
            false
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn elements_at_slot<'input, 'arena>(
    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
    combinators: &[Combinator],
    slot: usize,
    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
    other_mode: TypeMode,
) -> Vec<Element<'input, 'arena>> {
    root.breadth_first()
        .filter(|element| {
            matches_slot(
                element,
                compounds,
                combinators,
                slot,
                anchor_mode,
                other_mode,
            )
        })
        .collect()
}

fn analyze_selector<'input, 'arena>(
    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
    selector: &lightningcss::selector::Selector<'input>,
    protected: &mut HashSet<usize>,
    fallback: &mut ReferencedShapes,
) {
    let mut iter = selector.iter();
    let mut compounds: Vec<Vec<Component<'input>>> = Vec::new();
    let mut combinators: Vec<Combinator> = Vec::new();
    loop {
        compounds.push(iter.by_ref().cloned().collect());
        match iter.next_sequence() {
            Some(combinator) => combinators.push(combinator),
            None => break,
        }
    }

    let supported_combinator = |combinator: &Combinator| {
        matches!(
            combinator,
            Combinator::Child
                | Combinator::Descendant
                | Combinator::NextSibling
                | Combinator::LaterSibling
        )
    };
    if !combinators.iter().all(supported_combinator)
        || !compounds
            .iter()
            .all(|compound| compound.iter().all(is_supported_component))
    {
        for compound in &compounds {
            for component in compound {
                if let Component::LocalName(name) = component {

        ),
    )?);

    insta::assert_snapshot!(test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r##"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <polyline points="10,10 20"/>
    <polyline points="10,80 20,50 50,20 80,10"/>
    <polyline points="20 ,10  50    40 30.5-1e-1 , 20 10"/>
    <polyline stroke="#000" points="10,10 20,20 10,20"/>
    <polygon points="10,10 20"/>
    <polygon points="10,80 20,50 50,20 80,10"/>
    <polygon points="20 10  50 40 30,20"/>
    <polygon stroke="#000" points="10,10 20,20 10,20"/>
    <polygon stroke="none" points="10,10 20,20 10,20"/>
</svg>"##
        ),
    )?);

    insta::assert_snapshot!(test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": { "convertArcs": true } }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <circle cx="10" cy="10" r="5"/>
    <ellipse cx="10" cy="10" rx="5" ry="5"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?);

    insta::assert_snapshot!(test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": { "convertArcs": true, "floatPrecision": 3 } }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="65mm" height="45mm" viewBox="0 0 65 45">
  <rect x="26.614" y="29.232" width="34.268" height="8.1757"/>
  <line x1="26.6142" y1="29.2322" x2="34.2682" y2="8.1757"/>
  <polyline points="26.6142,29.2322 34.2682,8.1757"/>
  <polygon points="26.6142,29.2322 34.2682,8.1757"/>
  <circle cx="26.6142" cy="29.2322" r="34.2682"/>
  <ellipse cx="26.6142" cy="29.2322" rx="34.2682" ry="8.1757"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?);

    insta::assert_snapshot!(test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
  <defs>
    <rect id="rect1" width="120" height="120" />
  </defs>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?);


    // Converting either sibling would let `path + path` newly match, so both
    // siblings are protected.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r##"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>path + path { fill: red }</style>
    <rect width="10" height="10"/>
    <rect width="20" height="20"/>
</svg>"##
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "first sibling rect preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="20" height="20"/>"#), "second sibling rect preserved: {out}");

    Ok(())
}


#[test]
fn preserve_structure_dependent_selector_matches() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    use crate::test_config;

    // A structural selector only protects the specific relationship it matches;
    // unrelated shapes in the same document are still optimised.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>g > rect { fill: red }</style>
    <g><rect width="10" height="10"/></g>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "matched rect preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unrelated rect converted: {out}");

    // A selector mentioning `path` only blocks shapes that would newly match it.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>g > path { fill: blue }</style>
    <g><rect width="10" height="10"/></g>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "rect that would newly match preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unrelated rect converted (path selector): {out}");

    // A simple type selector protects every matching shape.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>rect { fill: red }</style>
    <rect width="10" height="10"/>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "all rects preserved for simple selector: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="30" height="30"/>"#), "all rects preserved for simple selector: {out}");

    // Compound selectors still scope protection to elements matching the full relationship.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>rect.foo { fill: red }</style>
    <rect class="foo" width="10" height="10"/>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect class="foo" width="10" height="10"/>"#), "matched compound preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unmatched rect converted (compound): {out}");

    // A shape used as an anchor (ancestor) is protected too.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>rect path { fill: red }</style>
    <rect width="10" height="10"><path d="M0 0"/></rect>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10">"#), "anchor rect preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unrelated rect converted (anchor): {out}");

    // Sibling relationships are taken into account.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>rect + path { fill: red }</style>
    <rect width="10" height="10"/><path d="M0 0"/>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "sibling anchor rect preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unrelated rect converted (sibling): {out}");

    Ok(())
}

+        "rect" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Rect),
+        "line" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Line),
+        "polyline" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Polyline),
+        "polygon" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Polygon),
+        "circle" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Circle),
+        "ellipse" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Ellipse),
+        _ => None,
+    }
+}
+
+fn is_supported_component(component: &Component<'_>) -> bool {
+    matches!(
+        component,
+        Component::LocalName(_)
+            | Component::ExplicitUniversalType
+            | Component::ExplicitAnyNamespace
+            | Component::ExplicitNoNamespace
+            | Component::DefaultNamespace(_)
+            | Component::Namespace(..)
+            | Component::ID(_)
+            | Component::Class(_)
+            | Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { .. }
+            | Component::AttributeInNoNamespace { .. }
+    )
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
+enum TypeMode {
+    /// Match against the element's actual local-name.
+    Real,
+    /// Match the anchor slot as if the element were already a `path`.
+    AsPath,
+    /// Match other slots as they will be after the rewrite: `path` type
+    /// selectors are ignored (they may match once converted shapes become
+    /// paths), while other type selectors are enforced.
+    IgnorePath,
+}
+
+fn compound_matches<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compound: &[Component<'input>],
+    mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    for component in compound {
+        match component {
+            Component::LocalName(name) => {
+                let selector_name = name.lower_name.as_ref();
+                let matches = match mode {
+                    TypeMode::Real => element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name,
+                    TypeMode::AsPath => selector_name == "path",
+                    TypeMode::IgnorePath => {
+                        selector_name == "path"
+                            || element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name
+                    }
+                };
+                if !matches {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::ID(ident) => {
+                if get_attribute!(element, Id).is_none_or(|id| id.0.as_str() != ident.as_ref()) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::Class(ident) => {
+                if !element.has_class(ident.as_ref()) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { local_name, .. } => {
+                let local_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
+                if element.get_attribute_local(&local_name).is_none() {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::AttributeInNoNamespace {
+                local_name,
+                operator,
+                value,
+                case_sensitivity,
+                ..
+            } => {
+                let attr_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
+                let Some(attr) = element.get_attribute_local(&attr_name) else {
+                    return false;
+                };
+                let value_str = attr
+                    .value()
+                    .to_value_string(PrinterOptions::default())
+                    .unwrap_or_default();
+                if !operator.eval_str(
+                    &value_str,
+                    value,
+                    case_sensitivity.to_unconditional(false),
+                ) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            // Universal selectors and namespace prefixes are ignored: changing a
+            // shape's local-name never affects whether those components match.
+            _ => {}
+        }
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+fn matches_slot<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    if !compound_matches(element, &compounds[slot], anchor_mode) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    matches_right(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
+        && matches_left(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
+}
+
+fn matches_right<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    if slot == 0 {
+        return true;
+    }
+    let next = slot - 1;
+    match combinators[slot - 1] {
+        Combinator::Child => element.children_iter().any(|child| {
+            compound_matches(&child, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_right(&child, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::Descendant => element.breadth_first().any(|descendant| {
+            compound_matches(&descendant, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_right(&descendant, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::NextSibling => element.next_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
+            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_right(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
+            let mut sibling = element.next_element_sibling();
+            while let Some(current) = sibling {
+                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                    && matches_right(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+                {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                sibling = current.next_element_sibling();
+            }
+            false
+        }
+        _ => false,
+    }
+}
+
+fn matches_left<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    if slot + 1 == compounds.len() {
+        return true;
+    }
+    let next = slot + 1;
+    match combinators[slot] {
+        Combinator::Child => element.parent_element().is_some_and(|parent| {
+            compound_matches(&parent, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_left(&parent, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::Descendant => {
+            let mut ancestor = element.parent_element();
+            while let Some(current) = ancestor {
+                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+                {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                ancestor = current.parent_element();
+            }
+            false
+        }
+        Combinator::NextSibling => element.previous_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
+            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_left(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
